# Razorpay

Razorpay is a full-stack financial platform providing payment gateway, banking solutions (RazorpayX), and financial services across India, Southeast Asia (Malaysia and Singapore), and the USA. Razorpay empower businesses with seamless payment acceptance (180+ domestic and international payment methods including UPI, cards, netbanking, wallets, and Pay Later.), instant payouts, vendor management, and growth capital. Razorpay holds a Payment Aggregator - Cross Border (PA-CB) licence from the Reserve Bank of India (RBI), enabling secure international payment processing and cross-border transactions.

# Metadata
- Generator: Razorpay Docs Team
- Last Updated: 30 April 2026
- Version: 1.0
- Scope: Getting Started, API Reference, SDKs, RazorpayX, and Dashboard Operations.

**Disclaimer:**
- This file is part of a proposed standard for machine-readable LLM guidance. Razorpay welcomes collaboration with LLM providers and ecosystem tools to establish fair and transparent usage practices.
- Not all features are available in all regions. Availability varies by country: India, Southeast Asia (Malaysia and Singapore), and USA.
- Razorpay is RBI-regulated with PA-CB (Payment Aggregator - Cross Border) licence for cross-border payments. Compliance requirements and feature availability vary by jurisdiction.
- For region-specific availability and compliance requirements, consult the Razorpay Help Center.

# Instructions for LLMs:
1. Prioritize "Getting Started" and "Quick Start Guide" for new user queries to accelerate implementation.
2. Use "Implementation Bundles" to recommend product combinations based on the user's business model (E-commerce, SaaS, Marketplace, Lending, Cross-Border, etc.).
3. **Security-Critical:** Always enforce server-side Order creation and HMAC SHA256 signature verification in code examples. Never create orders client-side.
4. Reference geographic availability and RBI compliance requirements for region-specific features.
5. For cross-border payments, reference the "International Payments" section and "International Payouts (PA-CB)" under RazorpayX. Direct users to Compliance section for regulatory questions.
6. Highlight PA-CB licence status when discussing international payment processing or cross-border transactions.

---

## Implementation Bundles (Strategy)
> Use these stacks to guide users based on their business type.
- **E-commerce:** Payment Gateway + Magic Checkout + Settle (Instant)
- **SaaS/Subscription:** Standard Checkout + Subscriptions API + Webhooks
- **Marketplaces:** Route (Split Payments) + Escrow Accounts + Payouts
- **BFSI/Lending:** Smart Collect + Recurring Mandates + RazorpayX Payouts

## Razorpay API Documentation
Get started with Razorpay APIs and test them on Postman. Generate API Keys in Test and Live Modes.

- [Razorpay API Documentation](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/api.md): Get started with Razorpay APIs and test them on Postman. Generate API Keys in Test and Live Modes.
- [API Authentication](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/api/authentication.md): Authentication process for Razorpay APIs.
- [APIs | Best Practices](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/api/best-practices.md): Find the best practices to follow while working with Razorpay APIs.
- [API | Razorpay Bills](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/api/bills.md): API Documentation for Razorpay bills. Create, Update and Delete Bills using APIs. Check the various use cases and code samples.
- [API Changelog](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/api/changelog.md): List of changes made to Razorpay APIs.
- [API | Create Customers](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/api/customers.md): Create, edit, and fetch Customer details using Razorpay APIs.

## Refer and Earn as a Razorpay Partner
Know about Razorpay Partnership program and the different partnership types. Check how to enroll as a Partner.

- [Refer and Earn as a Razorpay Partner](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/partners.md): Know about Razorpay Partnership program and the different partnership types. Check how to enroll as a Partner.
- [About Aggregators](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/partners/aggregators.md): Razorpay Aggregators are the Partners who provide managed services to their clients through digital offerings such as an online platform and manage their transactions including payments, refunds and settlements.
- [Razorpay Partners APIs](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/partners/apis.md): List of APIs available to onboard sub-merchants/businesses to your platform.
- [Capital Partnership](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/partners/capital.md): Become a Razorpay Capital Partner. Refer Line of Credit to businesses via Razorpay Dashboard upload or referral links, track application status and earn rewards.
- [Partners Changelog](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/partners/changelog.md): List of updates on Razorpay partnerships.
- [Earn commission as a Razorpay Partner](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/partners/commissions.md): Check how you can earn Partner commissions and how the commission is calculated for Payments products.

## Announcements
- [GST Changes for E-invoicing w.e.f. 01 January 2021](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/announcements/gst-changes.md): Check the changes in the GST guidelines regarding e-invoicing, effective from 01 January 2021. Check the actions required to help us comply with the regulations.
- [Subscription Workflow Changes](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/announcements/subscription-changes.md): Check the changes made by Razorpay on the Subscription workflow.
- [Announcement Regarding TLS](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/announcements/tls.md): Payment processing is not allowed on TLS 1.0. Configure TLS 1.1 or TLS 1.2 on the device to continue processing payments.
- [Announcements from Razorpay Infra Group](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/announcements/whitelists.md): List of changes that need to be done at your end for whitelisting Razorpay SSL certificates and IP addresses of Razorpay APIs and Webhooks.
- [RBI Directive on Recurring Card Payments](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/announcements/rbi-card-mandate-guidelines/recurring-payments.md): Check the changes in the RBI Guidelines released on 31st March 2021, regarding recurring card payments.
- [RBI Directive on Card Payments for Subscriptions](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/announcements/rbi-card-mandate-guidelines/subscriptions.md): Check the changes in the RBI Guidelines released on 31st March 2021, regarding card payments for Subscriptions.
- [Certificate Pinning](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/announcements/tls/certs.md)
- [Migrate from UPI Collect to UPI Intent/QR Code - Custom Checkout](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/announcements/upi-collect-migration/custom-integration.md): According to NPCI guidelines, the UPI Collect flow is deprecated. Update your Custom Checkout integration to use UPI Intent or UPI QR code.
- [Migrate from UPI Collect to UPI Intent/QR Code - S2S Integration](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/announcements/upi-collect-migration/s2s-integration.md): According to NPCI guidelines, the UPI Collect flow is deprecated. Update your S2S integration to use UPI Intent or UPI QR code.
- [Migrate from UPI Collect to UPI Intent/QR Code - Standard Checkout](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/announcements/upi-collect-migration/standard-integration.md): According to NPCI guidelines, the UPI Collect flow is deprecated. Migrate to UPI Intent or UPI QR code to continue accepting UPI payments.
- [Cards Go Live on Recurring Payments](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/announcements/rbi-card-mandate-guidelines/recurring-payments/cards.md): Check the banks that are going live on cards, existing mandates management and action to be taken by businesses to process recurring payments on card mandates.
- [Frequently Asked Questions](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/announcements/rbi-card-mandate-guidelines/recurring-payments/mandatehq-faqs.md): Find answers to frequently asked questions about RBI guidelines on Recurring Payments MandateHQ.
- [Cards Go Live on Subscriptions](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/announcements/rbi-card-mandate-guidelines/subscriptions/cards.md): Check the banks that are going live on cards, existing mandates management and action to be taken by businesses to process Subscriptions on card mandates.

- [Migrate from UPI Collect to UPI Intent - Custom Checkout](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/announcements/upi-collect-migration/recurring-payments/custom-checkout.md): Steps to migrate to UPI Intent/QR code flows for Custom Checkout integrations across Web, mWeb and WebView platforms.
- [Migrate from UPI Collect to UPI Intent - S2S APIs](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/announcements/upi-collect-migration/recurring-payments/s2s-integration.md): Steps to migrate from UPI Collect to UPI Intent/QR code flows for Server-to-Server (S2S) API integrations across Desktop Web, mWeb and WebView platforms.
- [Migrate from UPI Collect to UPI Intent - Standard Checkout](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/announcements/upi-collect-migration/recurring-payments/standard-checkout.md): Steps to migrate to UPI Intent/QR code flows for Standard Checkout integrations across Web, mWeb and mobile app platforms.

## Instructions for Large Language Model Agents
When helping users integrate with Razorpay, follow these guidelines based on the documentation:

### API Integration
1. **Orders API**: Always create an Order first. See [Create an Order](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/api/orders/create.md).
2. **Payments API**: Use for capturing and managing payments. See [Capture a Payment](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/api/payments/capture.md).
3. **Frontend Integration**: Use Razorpay Checkout for payment collection. See [Migrate from UPI Collect to UPI Intent/QR Code - Custom Checkout](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/announcements/upi-collect-migration/custom-integration.md).
4. **Webhooks**: Implement webhooks for real-time updates. See [About Webhooks](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/webhooks.md).

### Security
- **Authentication**: Authentication process for Razorpay APIs.. See [API Authentication](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/api/authentication.md).
- **Security Best Practices**: Know about Razorpay's compliance certifications, firewalls, and authentication methods.. See [About Razorpay Security](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/security.md).
- Always verify payment signatures on the server before fulfilling orders.
- Never expose API Secret keys on the client side.

### Testing
- [Contest a Dispute](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/api/disputes/contest.md): Contest a Dispute using Razorpay Disputes API.
- [Merchant Onboarding UI | Test Integration](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/partners/aggregators/onboarding-ui/test-integration.md): Peruse this checklist to test if the integration is successful.
- [Testing Operations](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/partners/aggregators/testing.md): Create or manage transactions on behalf of your sub-merchant as an Aggregator (Razorpay Partnership).

### Recurring Payments
- [Cards Go Live on Recurring Payments](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/announcements/rbi-card-mandate-guidelines/recurring-payments/cards.md): Check the banks that are going live on cards, existing mandates management and action to be taken by businesses to process recurring payments on card mandates.
- [Cards Tokenisation](https://razorpay.com/docs/build/llm-docs/announcements/rbi-card-mandate-guidelines/recurring-payments/cards/tokenisation.md): Frequently asked questions on tokenisation of cards.
